1: # LDO-CORE-SKY130
2: 
3: Design a continuous-time analog LDO meeting spec.json across conditions.json and the specified load range.
4: Create dut.spice with `.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF` and `.ends DUT`.
5: Choose connectivity, bias, compensation and dimensions yourself. No starting circuit is supplied.
6: Use only the declared MOS family and positive R/C in devices.md. Read measurement.md.
7: You have 90 minutes of cumulative model-call time with no native-case limit. Use the client to measure and submit your fixed circuit before the deadline.
8: No Web search. Use only the provided task information and your own knowledge.
9: Measurements consume cases even when simulation fails. Submit through the client with a rationale, then end your session.
10: 
11: ## Time accounting
12: 
13: The budget counts model calls, including provider queuing and transport. ngspice, Python, filesystem tools and their waits do not consume this budget. Model calls overlapping local tools still count. An already admitted measurement may finish after model-budget exhaustion; new design tools close. Use client status for the latest collected model-time reading.

1: # Shared client
2: 
3: Write request.json and call `atb-client --request request.json`.
4: Commands: `atb-client help`, `atb-client status`, `atb-client validate`,
5: `atb-client estimate --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
6: `atb-client measure --id m001 --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
7: `atb-client submit --rationale "Design rationale"`.
8: Use `--cl` in farads and optional `--seed` only when the task supports it.
9: The client reads dut.spice once for validate/measure/submit and sends its exact UTF-8 contents.
10: Measure requires experiment_id, analysis and pvt; cl is optional. Use help for analyses and native case costs.
11: Use a new experiment_id when changing a circuit or measurement request. Completed identical requests reuse their saved result.
12: Submit requires rationale. Successful submission freezes that circuit for every independent condition and closes exploration.
13: 
14: ## Public measurement options
15: 
16: | Analysis | PVT | Load (F) | Cases | Included native measurements |
17: |---|---|---:|---:|---|
18: | nominal | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-10 | 6 | dc_low, dc_mid, dc_high, light_heavy, mid_heavy, burst |

26: | dc_high | N, SLC, SLH, SHC, SHH, FLC, FLH, FHC, FHH | 2e-10 | 1 | dc_high |

40: 
41: Defaults and seed requirements:
42: 
43: - nominal: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
44: - dc: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
45: - dc_low: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
46: - dc_mid: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
47: - dc_high: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
48: - light_heavy: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
49: - mid_heavy: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
50: - burst: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
51: - line: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
52: - psrr_low: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
53: - psrr_high: omitted `--cl` uses 2e-10 F. No seed accepted.
54: 
55: One native case is one simulator process. Bundled internal analyses are retained in evidence.
56: `estimate` returns the exact case plan without launching a solver. Independent evaluation conditions are not available to participants.

Read file /workspace/measurement.md, lines 1-124
1: # Shared external measurements: LDO-CORE-2026-09-29
2: 
3: These definitions apply to both formal task cards. The implementation must be
4: checked natively with the pinned models; offline algebra is not that evidence.
5: Do not inherit the old fixed-OTA threshold or case-count definitions.
6: 
7: ## DC: port signs, cost and absolute voltage
8: 
9: All four DUT ports have zero-volt meters. Load and decap return to external node
10: `0`, not the DUT side of Vground. Thus the regulator ground-current measurement
11: does not include delivered load current.
12: 
13: `Iin=i(Vpin)`, `Iref=i(Vrefmeter)` enter VIN/VREF;
14: `Iout=i(Voutmeter)`, `Ignd=i(Vground)` leave VOUT/VSS.
15: Check `Iin+Iref-Iout-Ignd` against an absolute 1 nA tolerance. A failed residual is invalid measurement,
16: not a favorable low-current result. Save signed
17: currents, voltages and OP status. The DC overhead is
18: `max(Iin-Iout,0)+max(Iref,0)`, with a separate `abs(Iref)<=1 uA` gate. Reference
19: sourcing is charged, and sinking is already reflected in Iin-Iout. This is not
20: no-load IQ. Apply the minimum-load cap only at the minimum load, and the general
21: cap at all DC loads. Missing currents or invalid OP cannot become zero overhead.
22: Dissipation is `VIN*Iin+VREF*Iref-VOUT*Iout`, using VSS-relative voltages. Keep it
23: as characterization; input power contains useful load power. No dynamic-energy
24: score and no DC-overhead interpretation of capacitor charging during transients.
25: 
26: Measure error from each card's fixed target, not a candidate's own output:
27: SKY130 DC/recovery band is 1.176-1.224 V, envelope 1.104-1.296 V;
28: ADM45 DC/recovery band is 0.784-0.816 V, envelope 0.736-0.864 V.
29: Final verification evaluates independent DC solutions at every required load
30: and VIN condition. A transient tail is not a DC solution. Exploration may request
31: a single transient without separate DC cases: recovery uses the fixed target,
32: and the transient deck records its own operating point and initial hold. Such a
33: request does not establish DC compliance at every load.
34: Line/load regulation and actual signed currents remain available for diagnosis.
35: 
36: ## Prescribed loads and windows
37: 
38: | Profile | Levels | Time definition |
39: |---|---|---|
40: | light_heavy | card minimum -> maximum -> minimum | 1 us pre-hold, 100 ns edges, 2 us high/low plateaus |
41: | mid_heavy | card middle -> maximum -> middle | same |
42: | burst | minimum <-> maximum, 8 pulses | 100 ns edges, 200 ns high/low plateaus, 1 us extra final tail |
43: 
44: SKY130 levels are 50 uA/0.5 mA/2 mA; ADM45 levels 25 uA/0.25 mA/1 mA.
45: PWL loads are explicit electrical stimuli, not data from an RTL block. Reference
46: and VIN are fixed in load tests. The regulator stays enabled; start from its DC
47: solution without `.ic`/`uic`. The 1 us pre-hold must also satisfy the DC voltage
48: band; do not hide pre-existing oscillation by starting the score at the edge.
49: 
50: Recovery starts at each isolated edge START, not its end. Interpolate the last
51: entry into the target +/-2% band which stays in the band until the next edge
52: or record end. Require <=500 ns. Record extrema and absolute target-relative
53: errors during the whole transition/window. A flat but wrong voltage fails.
54: A complete trace ending outside the band has null recovery and an observation-
55: time lower bound; a bound exceeding the limit is MISS, not missing measurement.
56: Short/nonfinite/unordered/insufficiently sampled records are invalid evidence.
57: 
58: For bursts score the absolute envelope through **all** pulses, then recovery
59: only after the final falling edge. Do not apply a 500 ns per-edge settling
60: requirement to a 200 ns plateau. Total burst duration is 6.8 us, including its
61: extra tail. Record peak-to-peak output as characterization, not a new stability
62: score. Isolated profiles are 5.2 us. Positive and negative excursions both count.
63: 
64: Maximum timestep and requested output step are 0.5 ns. Check stored sample gaps
65: against 0.5 ns with relative slack 1e-9, using actual timestamps rather than
66: assuming the requested output grid was delivered. Qualify convergence and compare
67: representative extremes with a finer grid before release. This is finite-window,
68: sampled evidence, not proof against every internal or unobserved oscillation.
69: 
70: ## Line and PSRR: mandatory characterization, not scoring
71: 
72: Line: use each card's VIN minimum -> maximum -> minimum, 200 ns edges, maximum
73: load, reference fixed. Report the same target-relative windows. PSRR: valid
74: nominal OP at minimum and maximum load; VIN AC=1, reference/load AC=0.
75: `PSRR=20*log10(abs(VIN_ac/VOUT_ac))`. Save actual phasors at 1 kHz and 1 MHz and
76: the intervening sweep. This is supply-to-output rejection, not the OTA signal-
77: normalized ratio. Zero/underflow/nonfinite transfer is unresolved/invalid, not
78: infinite PASS. A non-regulating or oscillating state cannot provide a qualified
79: PSRR result merely because an AC command ran.
80: 
81: ## Models and accounting
82: 
83: The host selects the pinned SKY130 continuous `.lib` section or ADM45 generated
84: physical instances. ADM45 slow/fast uses process-only generation with the card's
85: fixed profile, seed and sample. Temperature is passed to the generator and bench;
86: the saved process values stay fixed across temperature, supply and load.
87: 
88: Each deck uses one native invocation, including its OP and subsequent
89: AC/tran commands. Standalone exploration transients cost one case each, including their operating
90: point and waveform. Separate DC requests cost one case per load point or three
91: for the complete DC set. The full nominal request costs six cases.
92: The formal plan is 18 public groups x 6 cases, 3 independent groups x 6 cases,
93: and 3 characterization cases = 129 native invocations. Each six-case group has
94: three DC destination OP cases plus light_heavy, mid_heavy and burst transients.
95: Initial OPs inside transient processes do not replace final verification DC cases.
96: The complete matrix is generated by the shared task planner.
97: Shared results can be reused only when the existing controller proves identity;
98: no cross-group count reduction is part of this specification. Keep every failed
99: or interrupted invocation; no free retries and no early stop on electrical MISS.
100: Independent evaluation uses the captured standard grid for every required case.
101: Numerical precision comparisons use a separately authorized developer allocation.
102: A standard-grid PASS is not a per-submission convergence proof.
103: 
104: Method sources (not support for our unmeasured numerical thresholds):
105: - https://ngspice.sourceforge.io/ngspice-control-language-tutorial.html
106: - https://www.analog.com/en/resources/analog-dialogue/articles/understand-ldo-concepts.html
107: - ADM45 VTG units, multiplicity and synthetic stress semantics are specified in the task card.
108: 
109: ## Verdict and observation completeness
110: 
111: Apply DC accuracy, current limits and KCL only to valid native OP records.
112: For each full transient, require the complete initial hold to be within the
113: absolute +/-2% band, and the post-edge envelope to stay within +/-8%. Isolated
114: profiles must recover by 500 ns from each edge start. Bursts apply recovery only
115: after the final falling edge. These are independent requirements: recovery does
116: not excuse a peak outside the envelope. Do not round values before inclusive
117: limit checks. A null recovery on a complete waveform carries its lower bound;
118: truncated, nonfinite, nonmonotone or overly sparse waveforms are invalid evidence.
119: 
120: Public and independent electrical cases must all have valid passing results for
121: an electrical PASS. Retain individual failing rows, observed waveforms and
122: execution status; do not turn incomplete execution into a completed electrical
123: MISS. Characterization is attempted and reported even when required metrics fail,
124: where simulation is possible; its unavailable results remain explicit.

1: # Netlist grammar
2: 
3: Use local .subckt/.ends, local .param and constant arithmetic + - * / in braces.
4: Elements are X for approved MOS wrappers or local subcircuits, R and C. No includes, models, sources or analyses.
5: MOS order is drain gate source body. NFET body connects to VSS; PFET body connects to VIN.
6: Use VSS instead of global node 0. Names are case-insensitive. Full-line * comments and + continuation are supported.
7: SKY130 W/L are bare numeric micrometres; nf=1 and integer m=1..32.
8: ADM45 W/L use SI or SPICE suffixes; integer mult=1..32, implicit nf=1, default ad/as/pd/ps=0.
9: R/C use SI or suffixes (M is milli, meg is mega). Positive R=1..1e8 ohm, C>=1e-15 F; total C and MOS W*L must satisfy spec.json.
10: Maximum source 65536 bytes, 2048 statements, 32 local subcircuits, depth 12, 2048 expansion visits, 512 expanded elements.
11: Only used subcircuits are allowed. Validation checks each physical copy and each technology's geometry domain.
12: 
13: Per-polarity domains (micrometres), intersect all tt/ss/ff sections and W<=100, L>=0.15:
14:

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Launch private OpenCode; optionally continue a verified truncated response."""
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import sqlite3
import subprocess
import sys

CONTINUE_MESSAGE = 'The previous response reached its output limit. Continue from where it stopped.'
POLICY = 'same-session-length-v1'


def saved_finish(session, message, database=None):
    """Read only this invocation's final assistant, never another session."""
    database = database or Path.home()/'.local/share/opencode/opencode.db'
    with sqlite3.connect(database.resolve().as_uri()+'?mode=ro', uri=True) as db:
        row = db.execute('SELECT data FROM session_message WHERE session_id=? AND id=? AND type=?',
                         (session, message, 'assistant')).fetchone()
    if row is None:
        raise ValueError('final assistant missing from private session')
    data = json.loads(row[0])
    if data.get('error') or not data.get('time', {}).get('completed'):
        raise ValueError('final assistant failed or did not settle')
    if data.get('finish') == 'length' and any(p.get('type') == 'tool' for p in data.get('content', [])):
        raise ValueError('truncated tool response requires review')
    return data.get('finish')


def terminal_assistant(session, observed_message, database=None):
    """CLI rendering can omit entire steps; use the settled private session tail."""
    database = database or Path.home()/'.local/share/opencode/opencode.db'
    with sqlite3.connect(database.resolve().as_uri()+'?mode=ro', uri=True) as db:
        row = db.execute('SELECT id FROM session_message WHERE session_id=? AND type=? '
                         'AND seq >= (SELECT seq FROM session_message WHERE session_id=? AND id=?) '
                         'ORDER BY seq DESC LIMIT 1',
                         (session, 'assistant', session, observed_message)).fetchone()
    if row is None:
        raise ValueError('private session tail missing or older than observed response')
    return row[0], saved_finish(session, row[0], database)


def receipt(decision, **fields):
    print(json.dumps(dict(type='atb_continuation', policy=POLICY, decision=decision, **fields)), flush=True)


def run(argv, prompt, maximum):
    continuations = 0
    session = None
    while True:
        latest = {}
        error = False
        args = ['/opt/opencode', *argv]
        if session:
            args += ['--session', session]
        # stdin is already consumed; EOF prevents CLI stdin from duplicating the message.
        with subprocess.Popen([*args, prompt], stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=subprocess.PIPE) as child:
            for line in child.stdout:
                sys.stdout.buffer.write(line)
                sys.stdout.buffer.flush()

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Participant measurement and submission client; authority stays with the broker."""
import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path
import socket
import sys


def parser():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser(prog='atb-client',description=__doc__)
    p.add_argument('--request',type=Path,help='Read a complete request from a JSON file')
    sub=p.add_subparsers(dest='command')
    for name in ('help','status','validate'):
        sub.add_parser(name)
    for name in ('estimate','measure'):
        s=sub.add_parser(name)
        if name=='measure':s.add_argument('--id',dest='experiment_id',required=True)
        s.add_argument('--analysis',required=True);s.add_argument('--pvt',required=True)
        s.add_argument('--cl',type=float,help='Load capacitance in farads')
        s.add_argument('--seed',type=int)
    sub.add_parser('submit').add_argument('--rationale',required=True)
    return p


def request_from(args,workspace):
    if args.request:
        if args.command:raise ValueError('--request cannot be combined with a command')
        request=json.loads(args.request.read_text())
    else:
        request={'action':args.command or 'status'}
        for key in ('experiment_id','analysis','pvt','cl','seed','rationale'):
            value=getattr(args,key,None)
            if value is not None:request[key]=value
    if not isinstance(request,dict):raise ValueError('Request must be a JSON object')
    if request.get('action') not in ('help','status','validate','estimate','measure','submit'):
        raise ValueError('Unknown participant action')
    if request['action'] in ('validate','measure','submit'):
        task=workspace/'task.json'
        if task.exists() and json.loads(task.read_text()).get('design_format')=='netlist':
            request['design']={'netlist':(workspace/'dut.spice').read_bytes().decode('utf-8')}
        else:request['design']=json.loads((workspace/'design.json').read_text())
    return request


def exchange(request,address):
    with socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX,socket.SOCK_STREAM) as sock:
        sock.connect(str(address))
        sock.sendall((json.dumps(request,allow_nan=False)+'\n').encode())
        with sock.makefile('r') as stream:
            reply=json.loads(stream.readline())
    if not isinstance(reply,dict):raise ValueError('Broker reply must be a JSON object')
    return reply


def main(argv=None,workspace=Path('/workspace'),address=Path('/run/atb/broker.sock')):
    args=parser().parse_args(argv)
    try:reply=exchange(request_from(args,Path(workspace)),address)
    except (OSError,ValueError,UnicodeError) as exc:
        # Do not include request contents, circuit bytes or provider credentials.
        reply={'ok':False,'error':type(exc).__name__,'reason':'Cannot read the request/design or exchange a valid broker reply.'}
    print(json.dumps(reply,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2,allow_nan=False))
    return 0 if reply.get('ok') is True else 1


if __name__=='__main__':sys.exit(main())

# Netlist grammar

Use local .subckt/.ends, local .param and constant arithmetic + - * / in braces.
Elements are X for approved MOS wrappers or local subcircuits, R and C. No includes, models, sources or analyses.
MOS order is drain gate source body. NFET body connects to VSS; PFET body connects to VIN.
Use VSS instead of global node 0. Names are case-insensitive. Full-line * comments and + continuation are supported.
SKY130 W/L are bare numeric micrometres; nf=1 and integer m=1..32.
ADM45 W/L use SI or SPICE suffixes; integer mult=1..32, implicit nf=1, default ad/as/pd/ps=0.
R/C use SI or suffixes (M is milli, meg is mega). Positive R=1..1e8 ohm, C>=1e-15 F; total C and MOS W*L must satisfy spec.json.
Maximum source 65536 bytes, 2048 statements, 32 local subcircuits, depth 12, 2048 expansion visits, 512 expanded elements.
Only used subcircuits are allowed. Validation checks each physical copy and each technology's geometry domain.

/workspace/loop_model.py
content
"""Small-signal two-node loop model for LDO architecture exploration.

Node A = error-amp output / pass gate (with optional buffer)
Node B = VOUT
Model:
  stage 0: ideal transconductor gm1 (EA) with output resistance R1a, driving node A
  optional buffer: gain (1) from node A to node G with output resistance Rb and cap Cg at node G
  pass device: gm2 from node G to node B (current into B = -gm2*vG)
  node B: g2 = 1/R2, C2 = CL
  feedback: beta (resistive divider, can include a lead cap)
  optional Miller/feedforward cap Cc from node G to node B
  optional reset/series Rz with Cc
"""
import numpy as np

def solve(T, beta, R1, C1, gm2, R2, C2, gm1, R3, C3, Cc, Rz, fbcap, R1f, R2f):
    """Return T(s) loop gain and closed loop response for the model.
    Chain: gm1 -> node A (R1,C1) -> optional buffer (gm_b: modeled as
    vG = -gm_b*R3*vA/(1+sR3C3) ... ) Simpler: two implementations below.
    """
    pass

def loop_2node(f, beta, gm1, R1, C1, gm2, R2, C2, Cc=0.0, Rz=0.0):
    """Single stage EA driving gate directly (node A==G), optional Miller cap w/ series Rz to output."""
    w = 2*np.pi*f
    s = 1j*w
    result = []
    for sv in s:
        # node A: i_ea = gm1*vin (into node A) ; pass current into B = -gm2*vA
        # cap branch A-B: z = Rz + 1/(sCc)
        g1 = 1.0/R1 + (1j*sv)*C1
        g2 = 1.0/R2 + (1j*sv)*C2
        if Cc > 0:
            yc = 1.0/(Rz + 1.0/(1j*sv*Cc))
        else:
            yc = 0.0
        # KCL A: gm1*vin = vA*(g1+yc) - vB*yc
        # KCL B: -gm2*vA = vB*(g2+yc) - vA*yc
        M = np.array([[g1+yc, -yc], [-gm2 - yc*0 + yc*0, g2+yc]])
        # rewrite: eq2: vB*(g2+yc) = vA*(yc - gm2)
        M = np.array([[g1+yc, -yc], [-(yc - gm2), g2+yc]])
        rhs = np.array([gm1*1.0, 0.0])
        sol = np.linalg.solve(M, rhs)
        vB = sol[1]
        T = -(beta*vB)/1.0   # negative of returned signal
        result.append(T)
    return np.array(result)

def pass_short(R1,C1,gm2,R2,C2,beta,gm1,fmin=1.0,fmax=1e11,npts=200000,Cc=0.0,Rz=0.0):
    f = np.logspace(np.log10(fmin), np.log10(fmax), npts)
    T = loop_2node(f,beta,gm1,R1,C1,gm2,R2,C2,Cc,Rz)
    mag = np.abs(T); ph = np.degrees(np.angle(T))
    # find unity gain crossings
    idx = np.where(np.diff(np.sign(mag-1.0)) != 0)[0]
    crossings = []
    for i in idx:
        f0 = f[i]
        # linear interp in log
        l0,l1 = np.log10(mag[i]), np.log10(mag[i+1])
        t = (0-l0)/(l1-l0)
        fx = 10**(np.log10(f[i]) + t*(np.log10(f[i+1])-np.log10(f[i])))
        phx = ph[i] + t*(ph[i+1]-ph[i])
        pagem = 180+phx
        crossings.append((fx,pagem))
    return f,mag,ph,crossings

if __name__ == '__main__':
    # Explore: which (gm1,Cc,...) gives PM>50deg at highest crossover, light load
    # light load: gm2=2.2mS ro=50k Rdiv=240k -> R2=41k ; heavy: gm2=11mS ro=1.7k -> R2=1.65k
    for (name,gm2,R2) in [('light',2.2e-3,41e3),('heavy',11e-3,1.65e3)]:
        print('====',name)
        for R1 in [1.2e6]:
            for C1 in [0.25e-12]:
                for gm1 in [100e-6,200e-6,400e-6,800e-6]:
                    for Cc in [0.0,1e-12,3e-12,10e-12]:
                        f,mag,ph,cr = pass_short(R1,C1,gm2,R2,200e-12,0.5,gm1,Cc=Cc,Rz=0.0,npts=20000)
                        if cr:
                            print(f"  R1={R1:.2g} C1={C1*1e12:.2f}p gm1={gm1*1e6:.0f}u Cc={Cc*1e12:.1f}p -> ", 
                                  " ".join(f"fu={c[0]/1e6:.2f}MHz PM={c[1]:.0f}" for c in cr[:2]))
                        else:
                            print(f"  R1={R1:.2g} gm1={gm1*1e6:.0f}u Cc={Cc*1e12:.1f}p -> no crossing, DCgain={np.abs(loop_2node(np.array([1e-3]),0.5,gm1,R1,C1,gm2,R2,200e-12,Cc,0.0))[0]:.0f}")

Now let me build a proper small-signal model to explore architectures before committing to a netlist.

write · loop2.py
/workspace/loop2.py
content
"""Two/three-node LDO loop model: EA + optional follower buffer + pass device.
Computes loop gain T(s), crossover/PM at light and heavy load, closed-loop load-step
response (with finite slew load), DC load regulation estimate.

Sign conventions: EA transconductor drives node A with current i_A = gm1*(beta*vO - vref)
(written as +gm1*beta*vO for the AC loop analysis). Pass device current into output node
is -gm2*vG (gate up -> less PMOS current)."""
import numpy as np

class Loop:
    def __init__(self, beta=0.5, gm1=250e-6, R1=500e3, C1=50e-15,
                 gm_b=250e-6, Cg=0.3e-12, gm2=3e-3, R2=40e3, C2=200e-12,
                 Cc=0.0, Rz=0.0, leadC=0.0, R1f=240e3, R2f=240e3):
        self.beta=beta; self.gm1=gm1; self.R1=R1; self.C1=C1
        self.gm_b=gm_b; self.Cg=Cg; self.gm2=gm2; self.R2=R2; self.C2=C2
        self.Cc=Cc; self.Rz=Rz; self.leadC=leadC; self.R1f=R1f; self.R2f=R2f

    def matrices(self, s):
        # nodes: A (EA out), G (gate), O (vout)
        G1=1/self.R1; G2=1/self.R2
        yc = 1.0/(self.Rz+1.0/(s*self.Cc)) if self.Cc>0 else 0.0
        # lead network in feedback: cap across R1f
        if self.leadC>0:
            Yf = 1/self.R1f + s*self.leadC   # top branch admittance (vout to fb)
            beta_s = (1/self.R2f)/(Yf*(1/self.R2f)+1)  # fb = vO * (1/R2f)/(Yf+1/R2f)
        else:
            beta_s = self.beta
        # MNA for [vA,vG,vO]
        M = np.zeros((3,3),dtype=complex); b=np.zeros(3,dtype=complex)
        # node A: gm1*(beta*vO - vref) = vA*(G1+sC1) - vG*0 ; plus buffer draws no input current
        M[0,0] = G1 + s*self.C1
        M[0,2] = -self.gm1*beta_s
        b[0] = -self.gm1      # vref input = 1
        # node G: gm_b*(vA-vG) = vG*sCg + yc*(vG-vO)
        M[1,0] = -self.gm_b
        M[1,1] = self.gm_b + s*self.Cg + yc
        M[1,2] = -yc
        # node O: -gm2*vG + vO*(G2+sC2) + yc*(vO-vG) = -i_load
        M[2,1] = -self.gm2 - yc
        M[2,2] = G2 + s*self.C2 + yc
        return M,b

    def loop_gain(self, f):
        s=1j*2*np.pi*f
        M,b=self.matrices(s)
        x=np.linalg.solve(M,b)   # vref=1, iload=0
        vO=x[2]
        # T(s) = beta * (returned signal); negative feedback -> T(0)>0
        return self.beta*vO

    def vout_transfer(self, f):
        # vO per vref (closed loop)
        return self.loop_gain(f)/self.beta

    def zout(self, f):
        s=1j*2*np.pi*f
        M,b=self.matrices(s)
        b2=np.zeros(3,dtype=complex); b2[2]=-1.0   # iload=1 A
        x=np.linalg.solve(M,b2)
        T=self.beta*np.linalg.solve(M,b)[2]
        return x[2]*(1+T)   # open-loop output impedance (for info)

    def zout_cl(self, f):
        s=1j*2*np.pi*f
        M,b=self.matrices(s)
        b2=np.zeros(3,dtype=complex); b2[2]=-1.0
        return np.linalg.solve(M,b2)[2]

    def report(self, verbose=True):
        f=np.logspace(0,11,40001)
        T=self.loop_gain(f)
        mag=np.abs(T); ph=np.degrees(np.angle(T))
        cr=[]
        idx=np.where(np.diff(np.sign(mag-1))!=0)[0]
        for i in idx:
            l0,l1=np.log10(mag[i]),np.log10(mag[i+1])
            t=(0-l0)/(l1-l0)
            fx=10**(np.log10(f[i])+t*(np.log10(f[i+1])-np.log10(f[i])))
            phx=ph[i]+t*(ph[i+1]-ph[i])
            cr.append((fx,180+phx))
        return cr

    def step_response(self, dI=1.95e-3, tr=100e-9, tmax=2.1e-6, dt=0.05e-9, CL=None):
        """Integrate linear system: C dv/dt = G v + b_ref*1 + b_iload*(-iL(t)).
        iL(t): 0 -> dI linear over tr.  Returns t, vO."""
        # state = [vA,vG,vO]; caps: diag(C1, Cg, C2+Cc etc) -- use MNA with s: G + s*Cmat
        Cmat=np.zeros((3,3))
        Cmat[0,0]=self.C1
        Cmat[1,1]=self.Cg+self.Cc
        Cmat[2,2]=self.C2+self.Cc
        Cmat[1,2]=-self.Cc; Cmat[2,1]=-self.Cc
        M0,_=self.matrices(1e-3)
        M1,_=self.matrices(1e-3)
        # G matrix = M(s) with s->0 contributions from conductances only
        def Gmat():
            G1=1/self.R1; G2=1/self.R2
            ycdc = 1.0/self.Rz if self.Cc>0 else 0.0
            if self.leadC>0:
                beta_s=0.5
            else:
                beta_s=self.beta
            G=np.zeros((3,3))
            G[0,0]=G1; G[0,2]=-self.gm1*beta_s
            G[1,0]=-self.gm_b; G[1,1]=self.gm_b+ycdc; G[1,2]=-ycdc
            G[2,1]=-self.gm2-ycdc; G[2,2]=G2+ycdc
            return G
        G=Gmat()
        bref=np.array([-self.gm1,0,0])
        def rhs(v,t):
            iL = 0.0 if t<=0 else (dI*min(t/tr,1.0))
            return G@v + bref*1.0 + np.array([0,0,-(-iL)])*0 + np.array([0,0,iL])
        # vO equation: node O KCL had "-i_load" on RHS. Load sinks current iL from node O,
        # so KCL: ... = -iL  => in G v = rhs form: rhs_O = -iL?  check sign below in tests.
        v=np.zeros(3); t=0.0; ts=[0.0]; vs=[0.0]
        A=np.linalg.inv(Cmat)
        while t<tmax:
            k1=A@rhs(v,t)
            k2=A@rhs(v+0.5*dt*k1,t+0.5*dt)
            k3=A@rhs(v+0.5*dt*k2,t+0.5*dt)
            k4=A@rhs(v+dt*k3,t+dt)
            v=v+dt/6*(k1+2*k2+2*k3+k4)
            t+=dt; ts.append(t); vs.append(v[2])
        return np.array(ts),np.array(vs)

def loads(R1f=240e3,R2f=240e3):
    return dict(beta=R2f/(R1f+R2f),R1f=R1f,R2f=R2f)

if __name__=='__main__':
    print("--- explore: EA+buffer, output pole dominant ---")
    for name,gm2,R2,C2 in [('light',3.0e-3,4.0e4,200e-12),('heavy',19e-3,1.6e3,200e-12)]:
        print('##',name)
        for A1 in [10,20,50,100]:
            for pA in [20e6,50e6,100e6,200e6]:
                gm1=250e-6; R1=A1/gm1; C1=gm1/(2*np.pi*pA*A1)  # pole at node A = gm1/(2pi A1 C1)=pA
                L=Loop(gm1=gm1,R1=R1,C1=C1,gm_b=300e-6,Cg=0.3e-12,gm2=gm2,R2=R2,C2=C2)

"""LDO loop model (scalar complex). Node A = EA output, G = pass gate, O = vout."""
import cmath, math

class Loop:
    def __init__(self, beta=0.5, gm1=250e-6, R1=500e3, C1=50e-15,
                 gm_b=250e-6, Cg=0.3e-12, gm2=3e-3, R2=40e3, C2=200e-12,
                 Cc=0.0, Rz=0.0, gm3=0.0, R3=None, C3=0.0):
        self.beta=beta; self.gm1=gm1; self.R1=R1; self.C1=C1
        self.gm_b=gm_b; self.Cg=Cg; self.gm2=gm2; self.R2=R2; self.C2=C2
        self.Cc=Cc; self.Rz=Rz
        self.gm3=gm3; self.R3=R3; self.C3=C3   # optional 2nd EA stage / extra node
    def T(self, f):
        s=1j*2*math.pi*f
        G1=1/self.R1; G2=1/self.R2
        yc = 1.0/(self.Rz+1.0/(s*self.Cc)) if self.Cc>0 else 0.0
        beta=self.beta
        # 3x3 solve for [vA,vG,vO] with vref=1, iload=0
        # (0): vA*(G1+sC1) - gm1*beta*vO = -gm1     [i_A = gm1*(beta vO - vref) into A]
        # (1): -gm_b*vA + vG*(gm_b+sCg+yc) - yc*vO = 0
        # (2): -gm2*vG - yc*vG + vO*(G2+sC2+yc) = 0
        a11=G1+s*self.C1; a12=0; a13=-self.gm1*beta
        a21=-self.gm_b; a22=self.gm_b+s*self.Cg+yc; a23=-yc
        a31=0; a32=-self.gm2-yc; a33=G2+s*self.C2+yc
        rhs=[-self.gm1,0,0]
        vO=solve3(a11,a12,a13,a21,a22,a23,a31,a32,a33,rhs)
        return beta*vO
    def Zout_cl(self, f):
        """closed loop vout for 1A load step (AC)."""
        s=1j*2*math.pi*f
        G1=1/self.R1; G2=1/self.R2
        yc = 1.0/(self.Rz+1.0/(s*self.Cc)) if self.Cc>0 else 0.0
        beta=self.beta
        a11=G1+s*self.C1; a12=0; a13=-self.gm1*beta
        a21=-self.gm_b; a22=self.gm_b+s*self.Cg+yc; a23=-yc
        a31=0; a32=-self.gm2-yc; a33=G2+s*self.C2+yc
        return solve3(a11,a12,a13,a21,a22,a23,a31,a32,a33,[0,0,-1.0])
    def crossings(self,fmin=1e2,fmax=2e10,n=4000):
        lf=[math.log10(fmin)+(math.log10(fmax)-math.log10(fmin))*i/(n-1) for i in range(n)]
        f=[10**x for x in lf]
        T=[self.T(x) for x in f]
        mag=[abs(t) for t in T]; ph=[math.degrees(cmath.phase(t)) for t in T]
        out=[]
        for i in range(n-1):
            if (mag[i]-1)*(mag[i+1]-1)<0:
                t=(0-mag[i])/(mag[i+1]-mag[i])
                fx=10**(lf[i]+t*(lf[i+1]-lf[i])); phx=ph[i]+t*(ph[i+1]-ph[i])
                out.append((fx,180+phx))
            if len(out)>=3: break
        return out
    def dc_gain(self):
        G1=1/self.R1; G2=1/self.R2
        T=self.T(1e-3)
        return abs(T)

def solve3(a11,a12,a13,a21,a22,a23,a31,a32,a33,b):
    det=(a11*(a22*a33-a23*a32)-a12*(a21*a33-a23*a31)+a13*(a21*a32-a22*a31))
    x2=(a11*(b[1]*a33-a23*b[2])-a12*(b[0]*a33-a23*b[2]*0-b[2]*a31*0+0)+a13*(b[1]*a31-a22*b[2]*0+0))
    # do full cramer
    import copy
    def det3(m):
        return (m[0][0]*(m[1][1]*m[2][2]-m[1][2]*m[2][1])
               -m[0][1]*(m[1][0]*m[2][2]-m[1][2]*m[2][0])
               +m[0][2]*(m[1][0]*m[2][1]-m[1][1]*m[2][0]))
    M=[[a11,a12,a13],[a21,a22,a23],[a31,a32,a33]]
    D=det3(M)
    Mv=[ [b[0],a12,a13],[b[1],a22,a23],[b[2],a32,a33] ]
    return det3(Mv)/D

def step_resp(L, dI, tr=100e-9, tmax=2e-6, dt=0.08e-9, nout=None):
    """Integrate C dv/dt = G v + bref + bload*iL(t).  Load sinks current at node O."""
    import numpy as np
    C=np.array([[L.C1,0,0],[0,L.Cg+L.Cc,-L.Cc],[0,-L.Cc,L.C2+L.Cc]])
    G=np.zeros((3,3))
    G1=1/L.R1; G2=1/L.R2
    ycdc = 1.0/L.Rz if L.Cc>0 else 0.0
    G[0][0]=G1; G[0][2]=-L.gm1*L.beta
    G[1][0]=-L.gm_b; G[1][1]=L.gm_b+ycdc; G[1][2]=-ycdc
    G[2][1]=-L.gm2-ycdc; G[2][2]=G2+ycdc
    bref=np.array([-L.gm1,0.0,0.0])
    Ci=np.linalg.inv(C)
    v=np.array([0.0,0.0,0.0])
    t=0.0; ts=[0.0]; vs=[0.0]
    while t<tmax:
        def rhs(v,t):
            iL = 0.0 if t<=0 else dI*min(t/tr,1.0)
            r=G@v+bref
            r[2]+= -iL
            return r
        k1=Ci@rhs(v,t); k2=Ci@rhs(v+0.5*dt*k1,t+0.5*dt)
        k3=Ci@rhs(v+0.5*dt*k2,t+0.5*dt); k4=Ci@rhs(v+dt*k3,t+dt)
        v=v+dt/6*(k1+2*k2+2*k3+k4); t+=dt
        ts.append(t); vs.append(v[2])
    return np.array(ts),np.array(vs)

if __name__=='__main__':
    print("sanity: gain sign & step direction")
    L=Loop(gm1=250e-6,R1=500e3,C1=50e-15,gm_b=300e-6,Cg=0.3e-12,gm2=3e-3,R2=40e3,C2=200e-12)
    print(" T(1mHz)=",L.T(1e-3), " |T|=",abs(L.T(1e-3)))
    print(" crossings:",[(f"{c[0]/1e6:.2f}MHz",f"{c[1]:.0f}deg") for c in L.crossings()])
    t,v=step_resp(L,1.95e-3)
    print(" step: min vO=%.3f V at t=%.1f ns"%(v.min(), t[v.argmin()]*1e9))

"""LDO loop model v2. State-space with explicit physical signs.

States: vA (EA output), vG (pass gate), vO (vout)
Equations / C:
  C1 dvA/dt = -vA/R1 + gm1*(beta*vO - VREF)
  Cg dvG/dt = gm_b*(vA - vG) - Cc*d(vG-vO)/dt      [cap current leaves G]
  C2 dvO/dt = -gm2*vG - vO/R2 - iL - Cc*d(vO-vG)/dt
i_A = gm1*(beta*vO - VREF)  (feedback to + input: negative loop with PMOS pass)
"""
import cmath, math

class Loop:
    def __init__(self, beta=0.5, gm1=250e-6, R1=500e3, C1=50e-15,
                 gm_b=250e-6, Cg=0.3e-12, gm2=3e-3, R2=40e3, C2=200e-12,
                 Cc=0.0, Rz=0.0):
        self.beta=beta; self.gm1=gm1; self.R1=R1; self.C1=C1
        self.gm_b=gm_b; self.Cg=Cg; self.gm2=gm2; self.R2=R2; self.C2=C2
        self.Cc=Cc; self.Rz=Rz

    def _mats(self, s, broken=False):
        """Return (G,C) such that s*C*v = G*v + b, with vref=1, iL=0 (AC).
        broken: loop opened (no beta feedback term)."""
        yc = 1.0/(self.Rz+1.0/(s*self.Cc)) if self.Cc>0 else 0.0
        # node A: vA*(1/R1+sC1) = gm1*(beta*vO - 1)  ; broken -> drop beta*vO
        # node G: -gm_b*vA + vG*(gm_b+sCg+yc) - yc*vO = 0
        # node O: -(gm2+yc)*vG + vO*(1/R2+sC2+yc) = 0
        n=3
        M=[[0j]*n for _ in range(n)]
        M[0][0]=1/self.R1+s*self.C1
        M[0][2]= 0.0 if broken else -self.gm1*self.beta
        M[1][0]=-self.gm_b; M[1][1]=self.gm_b+s*self.Cg+yc; M[1][2]=-yc
        M[2][1]=-self.gm2-yc; M[2][2]=1/self.R2+s*self.C2+yc
        rhs=[-self.gm1,0j,0j]
        return M,rhs

    def vO_cl(self,s):
        M,rhs=self._mats(s); v=solve3(M,rhs); return v[2]
    def vO_ol(self,s):
        M,rhs=self._mats(s,broken=True); v=solve3(M,rhs); return v[2]
    def T(self,f):
        s=1j*2*math.pi*f
        return -self.beta*self.vO_ol(s)   # negative return ratio: positive at DC
    def Zout_cl(self,f):
        s=1j*2*math.pi*f
        M,rhs=self._mats(s)
        rhs=[0j,0j,-1.0]
        v=solve3(M,rhs); return v[2]
    def crossings(self,fmin=1e2,fmax=5e10,n=6000):
        lf=[math.log10(fmin)+(math.log10(fmax)-math.log10(fmin))*i/(n-1) for i in range(n)]
        out=[]
        prev=None
        for i,x in enumerate(lf):
            f=10**x
            T=self.T(f); mag=abs(T)
            if prev is not None:
                if (prev[0]-1)*(mag-1)<0:
                    t=(1-prev[0])/(mag-prev[0])
                    fx=10**(prev[2]+t*(x-prev[2]))
                    phx=prev[3]+t*(math.degrees(cmath.phase(T))-prev[3])
                    out.append((fx,180-abs(phx)))
            prev=(mag,math.degrees(cmath.phase(T)),x,math.degrees(cmath.phase(T)))
            if len(out)>=3: break
        return out

def solve3(M,b):
    det=(M[0][0]*(M[1][1]*M[2][2]-M[1][2]*M[2][1])
        -M[0][1]*(M[1][0]*M[2][2]-M[1][2]*M[2][0])
        +M[0][2]*(M[1][0]*M[2][1]-M[1][1]*M[2][0]))
    def d3(m):
        return (m[0][0]*(m[1][1]*m[2][2]-m[1][2]*m[2][1])
               -m[0][1]*(m[1][0]*m[2][2]-m[1][2]*m[2][0])
               +m[0][2]*(m[1][0]*m[2][1]-m[1][1]*m[2][0]))
    out=[]
    for k in range(3):
        Mk=[row[:] for row in M]
        for r in range(3): Mk[r][k]=b[r]
        out.append(d3(Mk)/det)
    return out

def step_resp(L, dI, tr=100e-9, tmax=2e-6, dt=0.05e-9, vref=1.0, vout0=2.0):
    """Integrate closed loop with load step. Returns t, vO (deviation from initial)."""
    import numpy as np
    C=np.array([[L.C1,0,0],[0,L.Cg+L.Cc,-L.Cc],[0,-L.Cc,L.C2+L.Cc]])
    G=np.array([[ -1/L.R1,0,L.gm1*L.beta],
                [L.gm_b,-L.gm_b,0.0],
                [0.0,-L.gm2,-1/L.R2]],dtype=float)
    b=np.array([-L.gm1*vref,0.0,0.0])
    Ci=np.linalg.inv(C)
    v=np.array([0.0,0.0,0.0])
    t=0.0; out=[]
    while t<tmax:
        def rhs(v,t):
            iL=0.0 if t<=0 else dI*min(t/tr,1.0)
            r=G@v+b
            r[2]+= -iL
            return r
        k1=Ci@rhs(v,t); k2=Ci@rhs(v+0.5*dt*k1,t+0.5*dt)
        k3=Ci@rhs(v+0.5*dt*k2,t+0.5*dt); k4=Ci@rhs(v+dt*k3,t+dt)
        v=v+dt/6*(k1+2*k2+2*k3+k4); t+=dt
        out.append((t,v[2]))
    import numpy as np
    a=np.array(out)
    return a[:,0],a[:,1]

if __name__=='__main__':
    L=Loop(gm1=250e-6,R1=500e3,C1=50e-15,gm_b=300e-6,Cg=0.3e-12,gm2=3e-3,R2=40e3,C2=200e-12)
    print("closed-loop DC vO (VREF=1) =",L.vO_cl(1e-6),"  expect ~1/beta=2")
    print("T0=",abs(L.T(1e-3)),"  crossings:",[(f"{c[0]/1e6:.2f}MHz",f"{c[1]:.0f}deg") for c in L.crossings()])

"""Explore loop options numerically."""
import sys
sys.path.insert(0,'/workspace')
from loop2 import Loop, step_resp
import math

# pass device parameter model: gm2(I), ro(I) ; kp=60uA/V^2 pmos, lam ~ 0.3/L
def gm2(I, WL, kp=60e-6, nVt=0.033):
    vov = math.sqrt(2*I/(kp*WL))
    return 2*I/(vov+2*nVt)
def ro(I, lam=0.3/0.15):
    return 1.0/(lam*I)

def evald(gm1,R1,C1,gm_b,Cg,gm2v,R2,C2,Cc,verbose=False):
    L=Loop(gm1=gm1,R1=R1,C1=C1,gm_b=gm_b,Cg=Cg,gm2=gm2v,R2=R2,C2=C2,Cc=Cc)
    cr=L.crossings()
    return L,cr

if __name__=='__main__':
    print("== direct drive + Miller/feedforward cap Cc (node A = gate) ==")
    print("light: I=50u WL=1700 -> gm2=%.2fmS ro=%.0fk R2=ro||240k"%(gm2(50e-6,1700)*1e3, ro(50e-6)/1e3))
    for I,name in [(50e-6,'light'),(2e-3,'heavy')]:
        g=gm2(I,1700); r=ro(I, lam=0.3/0.15 if False else 2.0)  # lam=2 for L=0.15
        R2=1/(1/r+1/240e3); 
        print(f" {name}: gm2={g*1e3:.1f}mS ro={r/1e3:.1f}k R2={R2:.0f}")
        for gm1 in [200e-6,400e-6,800e-6]:
            for R1 in [300e3,1e6,3e6]:
                for Cc in [0,2e-12,10e-12]:
                    L,cr=evald(gm1,R1,0.4e-12,300e-6,0.4e-12,g,R2,200e-12,Cc)
                    T0=abs(L.T(1e-3))
                    s=f"T0={T0:8.0f} "+" ".join(f"{c[0]/1e6:.2f}MHz/{c[1]:.0f}deg" for c in cr[:2])
                    # also +ve crossover at 0dB with phase<180
                    print(f"   gm1={gm1*1e6:.0f}u R1={R1/1e3:.0f}k Cc={Cc*1e12:.0f}p {s}")

import sys; sys.path.insert(0,'/workspace')
from loop2 import Loop, step_resp
import numpy as np, math

def gm2(I, WL, kp=60e-6, nVt=0.033):
    vov = math.sqrt(2*I/(kp*WL)); return 2*I/(vov+2*nVt)

def show(tag, L, dI=1.95e-3):
    t,v=step_resp(L, dI, tmax=1.2e-6, dt=0.02e-9)
    i0=0
    # droop = max deviation below 0 after step
    mn=v.min(); mx=v.max(); tmn=t[v.argmin()]; tmx=t[v.argmax()]
    print(f"  {tag}: droop={-mn*1e3:7.1f}mV  overshoot={mx*1e3:7.1f}mV  (t_droop={tmn*1e9:.0f}ns t_ov={tmx*1e9:.0f}ns)")
    return t,v

# light load: gm2(50u), ro|Rdiv ; heavy: gm2(2m)
WL=1700
for name,I,lam in [('light',50e-6,2.0),('heavy',2e-3,2.0)]:
    g=gm2(I,WL); ro=1/(lam*I); R2=1/(1/ro+1/240e3)
    print(f"{name}: gm2={g*1e3:.2f}mS ro={ro:.0f} R2={R2:.0f}")

print("\n-- direct drive (no buffer): node A = gate, C1=Cg --")
for name,I,lam in [('light',50e-6,2.0)]:
    g=gm2(I,WL); ro=1/(lam*I); R2=1/(1/ro+1/240e3)
    for gm1 in [200e-6,400e-6]:
        for R1 in [300e3,1e6,3e6]:
            for Cc in [0,3e-12,10e-12]:
                L=Loop(gm1=gm1,R1=R1,C1=0.4e-12,gm_b=1.0,Cg=1e-15,gm2=g,R2=R2,C2=200e-12,Cc=Cc)
                cr=L.crossings()
                s=" ".join(f"{c[0]/1e6:.2f}MHz/{c[1]:.0f}d" for c in cr[:2]) or "none"
                print(f"  gm1={gm1*1e6:.0f}u R1={R1/1e3:.0f}k Cc={Cc*1e12:.0f}p T0={abs(L.T(1e-3)):7.0f} {s}")

Let me extend the model to the follower-drive architecture and check stability plus step response.

0:17:26
write · loop3.py
/workspace/loop3.py
content
"""4-node model: EA out (A) -> PMOS follower -> gate G -> pass -> out O, with feedforward cap Cc and
lead cap across R1f.  Load step response + loop gain PM."""
import numpy as np, math, cmath

class LDO:
    def __init__(s, beta=0.5, gm1=400e-6, R1=75e3, C1=30e-15,
                 gm_b=450e-6, Cg=1.1e-12, gm2=1.2e-3, R2=20e3, CL=200e-12,
                 Cc=0.0, Rz=0.0, Clead=0.0, R1f=300e3, R2f=300e3):
        s.beta=beta; s.gm1=gm1; s.R1=R1; s.C1=C1; s.gm_b=gm_b; s.Cg=Cg
        s.gm2=gm2; s.R2=R2; s.CL=CL; s.Cc=Cc; s.Rz=Rz; s.Clead=Clead
        s.R1f=R1f; s.R2f=R2f
    def _beta_s(s, sv):
        if s.Clead<=0: return s.beta
        # top branch: R1f || (1/sC) ; bottom R2f
        z1 = 1.0/(1.0/s.R1f + sv*s.Clead)
        return s.R2f/(z1+s.R2f)
    def _M(s, sv, broken=False):
        yc = 1.0/(s.Rz+1.0/(sv*s.Cc)) if s.Cc>0 else 0.0
        b = s._beta_s(sv) if not broken else 0.0
        M=np.zeros((3,3),dtype=complex)
        # A: vA*(1/R1+sC1) - gm1*b*vO = -gm1
        M[0,0]=1/s.R1+sv*s.C1; M[0,2]=-s.gm1*b
        # G: -gm_b*vA + vG*(gm_b+sCg+yc) - yc*vO = 0
        M[1,0]=-s.gm_b; M[1,1]=s.gm_b+sv*s.Cg+yc; M[1,2]=-yc
        # O: -(gm2+yc)*vG + vO*(1/R2+sCL+yc) = 0
        M[2,1]=-s.gm2-yc; M[2,2]=1/s.R2+sv*s.CL+yc
        return M, np.array([-s.gm1,0j,0j])
    def T(s,f):
        sv=1j*2*math.pi*f
        M,rhs=s._M(sv,broken=True)
        v=np.linalg.solve(M,rhs)
        return -s.beta*v[2]
    def crossings(s,fmin=1e2,fmax=3e10,n=4000):
        out=[]; prev=None
        for i in range(n):
            lf=math.log10(fmin)+(math.log10(fmax)-math.log10(fmin))*i/(n-1)
            f=10**lf; T=s.T(f); mag=abs(T); ph=math.degrees(cmath.phase(T))
            if prev and (prev[0]-1)*(mag-1)<0:
                t=(1-prev[0])/(mag-prev[0])
                fx=10**(prev[2]+t*(lf-prev[2])); phx=prev[3]+t*(ph-prev[3])
                out.append((fx,180-abs(phx)))
            prev=(mag,ph,lf,ph)
            if len(out)>=2: break
        return out
    def step(s,dI,tr=100e-9,tmax=1.5e-6,dt=0.01e-9):
        C=np.array([[s.C1,0,0],[0,s.Cg+s.Cc,-s.Cc],[0,-s.Cc,s.CL+s.Cc]])
        G=np.zeros((3,3)); bref=np.zeros(3)
        b=s.beta
        G[0,0]=-1/s.R1; G[0,2]=s.gm1*b; bref[0]=-s.gm1
        G[1,0]=s.gm_b; G[1,1]=-s.gm_b
        G[2,1]=-s.gm2; G[2,2]=-1/s.R2
        Ci=np.linalg.inv(C)
        v=np.zeros(3); t=0.0; out=[]
        while t<tmax:
            def rhs(v,t):
                iL=0.0 if t<=0 else dI*min(t/tr,1.0)
                r=G@v+bref; r[2]-=iL; return r
            k1=Ci@rhs(v,t); k2=Ci@rhs(v+0.5*dt*k1,t+0.5*dt)
            k3=Ci@rhs(v+0.5*dt*k2,t+0.5*dt); k4=Ci@rhs(v+dt*k3,t+dt)
            v=v+dt/6*(k1+2*k2+2*k3+k4); t+=dt
            out.append((t,v[2]))
        a=np.array(out); return a[:,0],a[:,1]

def gm2m(I, WL, kp=60e-6, nVt=0.033):
    vov=math.sqrt(2*I/(kp*WL)); return 2*I/(vov+2*nVt)

if __name__=='__main__':
    for WL in [1500,5000]:
        print(f"=== pass W/L={WL}: gm2(50u)={gm2m(50e-6,WL)*1e3:.2f}mS gm2(2m)={gm2m(2e-3,WL)*1e3:.2f}mS")
    # light load condition
    for tag,I,lam,gm1,C1,gmb,Cg in [('light',50e-6,1.0,400e-6,30e-15,450e-6,1.1e-12)]:
        g=gm2m(I,5000); ro=1/(lam*I); R2=1/(1/ro+1/600e3)
        print(f"\n{tag}: gm2={g*1e3:.2f}mS ro={ro/1e3:.1f}k R2={R2/1e3:.1f}k T0={0.5*(gm1*75e3)*g*R2:.0f}")
        for Cc in [0,3e-12,10e-12,15e-12]:
            L=LDO(gm1=gm1,R1=75e3,C1=C1,gm_b=gmb,Cg=Cg,gm2=g,R2=R2,CL=200e-12,Cc=Cc)
            cr=L.crossings()
            t,v=L.step(1.95e-3)
            s=" ".join(f"{c[0]/1e6:.1f}MHz/{c[1]:.0f}d" for c in cr) or "none"
            print(f"  Cc={Cc*1e12:4.0f}p fu/PM: {s:20s} droop={-v.min()*1e3:7.1f}mV")

import sys; sys.path.insert(0,'/workspace')
from loop3 import LDO, gm2m
import numpy as np, math

def cond(I, WL, lam, Rdiv=600e3, CL=200e-12):
    g=gm2m(I,WL); ro=1/(lam*I); R2=1/(1/ro+1/Rdiv)
    return g,R2

best=[]
for WL in [1500,5000,10000]:
    for gm1 in [200e-6,300e-6,400e-6,600e-6]:
        for C1 in [10e-15,20e-15,40e-15]:
            for gmb in [300e-6,600e-6,900e-6]:
                for lam in [0.5,1.0,2.0]:
                    ok=True; rows=[]
                    for I,tag in [(50e-6,'L'),(2e-3,'H')]:
                        g,R2=cond(I,WL,lam)
                        L=LDO(gm1=gm1,R1=30e-6*0+gm1 and (30/gm1),C1=C1,gm_b=gmb,Cg=WL*0.15*8.4e-15/1000+0.2e-15*WL/1e3*0+WL*0.15*8.4e-15*0+ (WL*0.15*8.4e-6*1e-6 if False else (WL*0.15)*8.4e-15)+ (0.2e-15*WL/1e3)*0 ,gm2=g,R2=R2,CL=CL,Cc=0.0)
                        # Cg = area*8.4fF/um^2 + overlap
                        Cg=(WL*0.15)*8.4e-15 + 0.2e-15*(WL*0.15/0.15)/1e3*1e3
                        L.Cg=Cg
                        cr=L.crossings()
                        if not cr: ok=False; break
                        fu,pm=cr[0]
                        t,v=L.step(1.95e-3,tmax=1.2e-6,dt=0.02e-9)
                        droop=-v.min()*1e3; ov=v.max()*1e3
                        rows.append((tag,Cg*1e12,fu/1e6,pm,droop,ov))
                    if ok:
                        worst=max(r[4] for r in rows); worso=max(r[5] for r in rows)
                        best.append((max(worst,worso),WL,gm1,C1,gmb,lam,rows))
best.sort()
for b in best[:18]:
    print(f"score={b[0]:7.1f} WL={b[1]:5d} gm1={b[2]*1e6:5.0f}u C1={b[3]*1e15:4.0f}f gmb={b[4]*1e6:5.0f}u lam={b[5]:3.1f}")
    for r in b[6]:
        print(f"     {r[0]}: Cg={r[1]:5.2f}p fu={r[2]:6.1f}MHz PM={r[3]:5.0f} droop={r[4]:7.1f}mV ov={r[5]:7.1f}mV")

import sys; sys.path.insert(0,'/workspace')
from loop3 import LDO, gm2m
import numpy as np, math

def cond(I, WL, lam, Rdiv=600e3, CL=200e-12):
    g=gm2m(I,WL); ro=1/(lam*I); R2=1/(1/ro+1/Rdiv); return g,R2

def Cg_of(WL, L=0.15):
    return (WL*L)*8.4e-15 + 0.2e-15*(WL*L/L)

best=[]
CL=200e-12
for WL in [1500,5000,10000,20000]:
    for gm1 in [200e-6,300e-6,450e-6,650e-6]:
        for A1 in [10,20,40]:
            for C1 in [10e-15,25e-15,50e-15]:
                for gmb in [300e-6,600e-6,1000e-6]:
                    for lam in [0.5,1.0]:
                        ok=True; rows=[]
                        for I,tag in [(50e-6,'L'),(2e-3,'H')]:
                            g,R2=cond(I,WL,lam,CL=CL)
                            L=LDO(gm1=gm1,R1=A1/gm1,C1=C1,gm_b=gmb,Cg=Cg_of(WL),gm2=g,R2=R2,CL=CL,Cc=0.0)
                            cr=L.crossings()
                            if not cr: ok=False; break
                            fu,pm=cr[0]
                            t,v=L.step(1.95e-3,tmax=1.2e-6,dt=0.02e-9)
                            rows.append((tag,fu/1e6,pm,-v.min()*1e3,v.max()*1e3))
                        if ok:
                            w=max(max(r[3] for r in rows), max(r[4] for r in rows))
                            best.append((w,WL,gm1,A1,C1,gmb,lam,rows))
best.sort(key=lambda b:b[0])
for b in best[:20]:
    print(f"worst={b[0]:7.1f}mV WL={b[1]:6d} gm1={b[2]*1e6:4.0f}u A1={b[3]:2d} C1={b[4]*1e15:3.0f}f gmb={b[5]*1e6:5.0f}u lam={b[6]:.1f}  " +
          " | ".join(f"{r[0]}: fu={r[1]:5.1f}M PM={r[2]:3.0f} dr={r[3]:6.1f} ov={r[4]:6.1f}" for r in b[7]))

"""3-node: EA(A) -> inverting driver -> gate(G) -> pass -> out(O); optional Cc G-O."""
import numpy as np, math, cmath
class L3:
    def __init__(s, beta=0.5, gm1=400e-6, RA=200e3, CA=40e-15, gmd=400e-6, Rg=300e3,
                 Cg=1.16e-12, gm2=1.2e-3, R2=19e3, CL=200e-12, Cc=0.0, Rz=0.0):
        s.beta=beta;s.gm1=gm1;s.RA=RA;s.CA=CA;s.gmd=gmd;s.Rg=Rg;s.Cg=Cg;s.gm2=gm2;s.R2=R2;s.CL=CL;s.Cc=Cc;s.Rz=Rz
    def _M(s,sv,broken=False):
        yc = 1.0/(s.Rz+1.0/(sv*s.Cc)) if s.Cc>0 else 0.0
        b = 0.0 if broken else s.beta
        M=np.zeros((3,3),dtype=complex)
        # A: vA*(1/RA+sCA) + gm1*b*vO = -gm1  (i_A=gm1*(vref-beta vO) into A; loop neg with driver inversion)
        M[0,0]=1/s.RA+sv*s.CA; M[0,2]= s.gm1*b
        # G: -gmd*vA + vG*(1/Rg+sCg+yc) - yc*vO = 0
        M[1,0]=-s.gmd; M[1,1]=1/s.Rg+sv*s.Cg+yc; M[1,2]=-yc
        # O: -(gm2+yc)*vG + vO*(1/R2+sCL+yc)=0
        M[2,1]=-s.gm2-yc; M[2,2]=1/s.R2+sv*s.CL+yc
        return M, np.array([-s.gm1,0j,0j])
    def T(s,f):
        sv=1j*2*math.pi*f; M,rhs=s._M(sv,broken=True)
        v=np.linalg.solve(M,rhs); return -s.beta*v[2]
    def crossings(s,fmin=10,fmax=2e10,n=3000):
        out=[]; prev=None
        for i in range(n):
            lf=math.log10(fmin)+(math.log10(fmax)-math.log10(fmin))*i/(n-1); f=10**lf
            T=s.T(f); mag=abs(T); ph=math.degrees(cmath.phase(T))
            if prev and (prev[0]-1)*(mag-1)<0:
                t=(1-prev[0])/(mag-prev[0]); fx=10**(prev[2]+t*(lf-prev[2])); phx=prev[3]+t*(ph-prev[3])
                out.append((fx,180-abs(phx)))
                if len(out)>=2: break
            prev=(mag,ph,lf,ph)
        return out
    def step(s,dI,tr=100e-9,tmax=1.2e-6,dt=0.01e-9):
        C=np.array([[s.CA,0,0],[0,s.Cg+s.Cc,-s.Cc],[0,-s.Cc,s.CL+s.Cc]])
        G=np.zeros((3,3)); bref=np.zeros(3); b=s.beta
        G[0,0]=-1/s.RA; G[0,2]=-s.gm1*b           # i_A=gm1*(vref-b*vO) -> w/ driver inversion the loop is negative
        G[1,0]=-s.gmd; G[1,1]=-1/s.Rg
        G[2,1]=-s.gm2; G[2,2]=-1/s.R2
        Ci=np.linalg.inv(C); v=np.zeros(3); t=0.0; out=[]
        while t<tmax:
            def rhs(v,t):
                iL=0.0 if t<=0 else dI*min(t/tr,1.0)
                r=G@v; r[2]-=iL; return r
            k1=Ci@rhs(v,t); k2=Ci@rhs(v+0.5*dt*k1,t+0.5*dt)
            k3=Ci@rhs(v+0.5*dt*k2,t+0.5*dt); k4=Ci@rhs(v+dt*k3,t+dt)
            v=v+dt/6*(k1+2*k2+2*k3+k4); t+=dt
            out.append((t,v[2]))
        a=np.array(out); return a[:,0],a[:,1]

def gm2m(I, WL, kp=60e-6, nVt=0.033):
    vov=math.sqrt(2*I/(kp*WL)); return 2*I/(vov+2*nVt)

if __name__=='__main__':
    import itertools
    res=[]
    for WL in [5333]:
      for gm1 in [300e-6,600e-6]:
       for A_ea in [50,150,400]:
        for CA in [30e-15,60e-15]:
         for gmd in [200e-6,500e-6]:
          for Rg in [100e3,300e3,1e6]:
           for Cc in [0,5e-12,12e-12]:
            rows=[];ok=True
            for I in [50e-6,2e-3]:
                g=gm2m(I,WL); ro=1/(0.5*I); R2=1/(1/ro+1/200e3)
                L=L3(gm1=gm1,RA=A_ea/gm1,CA=CA,gmd=gmd,Rg=Rg,Cg=(WL*0.15)*8.4e-15+0.2e-15*(WL*0.15/0.15),gm2=g,R2=R2,CL=200e-12,Cc=Cc)
                cr=L.crossings()
                if not cr: ok=False;break
                fu,pm=cr[0]
                t,v=L.step(1.95e-3)
                rows.append((fu/1e6,pm,-v.min()*1e3,v.max()*1e3))
            if ok and min(r[1] for r in rows)>25:
                res.append((max(max(r[2],r[3]) for r in rows),WL,gm1,A_ea,CA,gmd,Rg,Cc,rows))
    res.sort(key=lambda x:x[0])
    for r in res[:15]:
        print(f"score={r[0]:6.1f} gm1={r[2]*1e6:4.0f}u A={r[3]:3d} CA={r[4]*1e15:2.0f}f gmd={r[5]*1e6:4.0f}u Rg={r[6]/1e3:5.0f}k Cc={r[7]*1e12:2.0f}p | " +
              " | ".join(f"fu={x[0]:5.1f}M PM={x[1]:3.0f} dr={x[2]:6.1f} ov={x[3]:6.1f}" for x in r[8]))
    print(len(res),"configs with PM>25")

content
"""3-node: EA(A) -> driver(-A_drv) -> gate(G) -> pass -> out(O);
Cc from A to O (fast feedforward / Miller across driver+pass).  Also Cc2 from G to O."""
import numpy as np, math, cmath
class L4:
    def __init__(s, beta=0.5, gm1=400e-6, RA=133e3, CA=40e-15, A_drv=60.0, Rg=250e3,
                 Cg=73e-15, gm2=1.2e-3, R2=20e3, CL=200e-12, Cc=0.0, Cc2=0.0):
        s.beta=beta;s.gm1=gm1;s.RA=RA;s.CA=CA;s.A_drv=A_drv;s.Rg=Rg;s.Cg=Cg;s.gm2=gm2;s.R2=R2;s.CL=CL;s.Cc=Cc;s.Cc2=Cc2
    def _M(s,sv,broken=False):
        b = 0.0 if broken else s.beta
        M=np.zeros((3,3),dtype=complex)
        yc=sv*s.Cc; yc2=sv*s.Cc2
        # node A: vA*(1/RA+sCA+yc) + gm1*b*vO = -gm1   (i_A=gm1*(vref-b vO) into A)
        M[0,0]=1/s.RA+sv*s.CA+yc; M[0,2]=s.gm1*b - yc
        # node G: -A_drv*vA/... use transconductance model: i_g = gmd*vA ; gmd=A_drv*(1/Rg?) -> use gmd=Rg model
        gmd = s.A_drv/s.Rg
        M[1,0]=-gmd; M[1,1]=1/s.Rg+sv*s.Cg+yc2; M[1,2]=-yc2
        # node O: -(gm2+yc2)*vG + vO*(1/R2+sCL+yc2+yc) - yc*vA = 0  ; careful sign for Cc: current leaves O into A
        M[2,0]=-yc; M[2,1]=-s.gm2-yc2; M[2,2]=1/s.R2+sv*s.CL+yc2+yc
        return M, np.array([-s.gm1,0j,0j])
    def T(s,f):
        sv=1j*2*math.pi*f; M,rhs=s._M(sv,broken=True)
        v=np.linalg.solve(M,rhs); return -s.beta*v[2]
    def crossings(s,fmin=10,fmax=2e10,n=2500):
        out=[]; prev=None
        for i in range(n):
            lf=math.log10(fmin)+(math.log10(fmax)-math.log10(fmin))*i/(n-1); f=10**lf
            T=s.T(f); mag=abs(T); ph=math.degrees(cmath.phase(T))
            if prev and (prev[0]-1)*(mag-1)<0:
                t=(1-prev[0])/(mag-prev[0]); fx=10**(prev[2]+t*(lf-prev[2])); phx=prev[3]+t*(ph-prev[3])
                out.append((fx,180-abs(phx)))
                if len(out)>=2: break
            prev=(mag,ph,lf,ph)
        return out
    def step(s,dI,tr=100e-9,tmax=1.2e-6,dt=0.01e-9):
        Ca=np.array([[s.CA+s.Cc,0,-s.Cc],[0,s.Cg+s.Cc2,-s.Cc2],[-s.Cc,-s.Cc2,s.CL+s.Cc+s.Cc2]])
        G=np.zeros((3,3)); b=s.beta
        gmd=s.A_drv/s.Rg
        G[0,0]=-1/s.RA; G[0,2]=-s.gm1*b
        G[1,0]=gmd; G[1,1]=-1/s.Rg
        G[2,1]=-s.gm2; G[2,2]=-1/s.R2
        Ci=np.linalg.inv(Ca); v=np.zeros(3); t=0.0; out=[]
        while t<tmax:
            def rhs(v,t):
                iL=0.0 if t<=0 else dI*min(t/tr,1.0)
                r=G@v; r[2]-=iL; return r
            k1=Ci@rhs(v,t); k2=Ci@rhs(v+0.5*dt*k1,t+0.5*dt)
            k3=Ci@rhs(v+0.5*dt*k2,t+0.5*dt); k4=Ci@rhs(v+dt*k3,t+dt)
            v=v+dt/6*(k1+2*k2+2*k3+k4); t+=dt
            out.append((t,v[2]))
        a=np.array(out); return a[:,0],a[:,1]
def gm2m(I, WL, kp=60e-6, nVt=0.033):
    vov=math.sqrt(2*I/(kp*WL)); return 2*I/(vov+2*nVt)
if __name__=='__main__':
    res=[]
    for gm1 in [300e-6,500e-6]:
     for A_ea in [20,50,120]:
      for CA in [30e-15,60e-15]:
       for Adv in [15,40,100]:
        for Rg in [100e3,300e3]:
         for Cc in [0,1e-12,3e-12,10e-12]:
          rows=[];ok=True
          for I in [50e-6,2e-3]:
            g=gm2m(I,333); ro=1/(0.8*I); R2=1/(1/ro+1/100e3)
            L=L4(gm1=gm1,RA=A_ea/gm1,CA=CA,A_drv=Adv,Rg=Rg,Cg=73e-15,gm2=g,R2=R2,CL=200e-12,Cc=Cc)
            cr=L.crossings()
            if not cr: ok=False;break
            fu,pm=cr[0]
            t,v=L.step(1.95e-3,tmax=0.8e-6,dt=0.02e-9)
            if abs(v).max()>0.4: ok=False;break
            rows.append((fu/1e6,pm,-v.min()*1e3,v.max()*1e3))
          if ok and min(r[1] for r in rows)>35:
            res.append((max(max(r[2],r[3]) for r in rows),gm1,A_ea,CA,Adv,Rg,Cc,rows))
    res.sort(key=lambda x:x[0])
    for r in res[:12]:
        print(f"score={r[0]:6.1f} gm1={r[1]*1e6:4.0f}u Aea={r[2]:3d} CA={r[3]*1e15:2.0f}f Adv={r[4]:3d} Rg={r[5]/1e3:3.0f}k Cc={r[6]*1e12:4.1f}p | "+" | ".join(f"fu={x[0]:5.1f}M PM={x[1]:3.0f} dr={x[2]:6.1f} ov={x[3]:6.1f}" for x in r[7]))
    print(len(res),"configs")

/workspace/gen.py
content
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Generate dut.spice variants and run client measurements."""
import subprocess, json, sys, os

TMPL = """.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
* pass device
XMP VOUT VG VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={wp} L={lp} m={mp}
* error amp (PMOS pair, NMOS mirror, PMOS tail)
XM1 na {g1} st VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={w1} L={l1} m={m1}
XM2 nb {g2} st VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={w1} L={l1} m={m1}
XM3 na na VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W={w3} L={l3} m=1
XM4 nb na VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W={w3} L={l3} m=1
XM5 st nbp VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={w5} L={l5} m={m5}
* gate driver
XMN VG nb VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W={wn} L={ln} m={mn}
XMP2 VG nb VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={wpu} L={lpu} m={mpu}
* bias
RB VIN nbias {rb}
XM8 nbias nbias VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W=10 L=1 m=1
XM9 nbp nbias VSS VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 W={w9} L=1 m={m9}
XM6 nbp nbp VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W={w6} L=1 m={m6}
* feedback divider
R1 VOUT VFB {r1}
R2 VFB VSS {r1}
{caps}.ends DUT
"""

P = dict(wp=100,lp=0.15,mp=1, w1=20,l1=0.2,m1=1, w3=10,l3=0.5, w5=20,l5=0.5,m5=2,
         wn=1,ln=0.5,mn=1, wpu=3,lpu=0.5,mpu=1, rb='300k', w9=10,m9=2, w6=20,m6=1,
         r1='100k', g1='VREF', g2='VFB', caps='')

def build(over=None):
    p=dict(P); p.update(over or {})
    return TMPL.format(**p)

def run(args):
    r=subprocess.run(['atb-client']+args,capture_output=True,text=True)
    try: return json.loads(r.stdout)
    except Exception: return {'raw':r.stdout,'err':r.stderr}

def measure(case,over,analysis='dc',pvt='N',short=False):
    open('/workspace/dut.spice','w').write(build(over))
    req={'action':'measure','analysis':analysis,'pvt':pvt,'experiment_id':case}
    open('/workspace/req.json','w').write(json.dumps(req))
    rep=run(['--request','/workspace/req.json'])
    if short:
        out=[]
        for c in rep.get('cases',[]):
            m=c.get('measurements',{})
            out.append((c['case_id'], c['status'], m.get('vout_v'), m.get('overhead_a'), c.get('solver_status')))
        print(case,out)
    else:
        print(json.dumps(rep,indent=1)[:4000])
    return rep

if __name__=='__main__':
    which=sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv)>1 else 'A'
    if which=='A':
        measure('v2A',{'g1':'VREF','g2':'VFB'},short=True)   # inverter + VFB on M2
    elif which=='B':
        measure('v2B',{'g1':'VFB','g2':'VREF'},short=True)   # inverter + VFB on M1

v2B [('dc_low', 'OK', 1.597809917948083, 2.694492756794703e-05, 'OK'), ('dc_mid', 'MEASUREMENT_FAILURE', None, None, 'OK'), ('dc_high', 'MEASUREMENT_FAILURE', None, None, 'OK')]

from gen import measure
def summ(rep):
    for c in rep.get('cases',[]):
        m=c.get('measurements',{})
        line=[c['case_id'], c['status']]
        if 'vout_v' in m: line.append('vout=%.4f'%m['vout_v'])
        if 'overhead_a' in m: line.append('ovh=%.1fuA'%(m['overhead_a']*1e6))
        ih=m.get('initial_hold')
        if ih: line.append('hold=[%.4f,%.4f]'%(ih['min_v'],ih['max_v']))
        en=m.get('envelope')
        if en: line.append('env=[%.4f,%.4f]'%(en['min_v'],en['max_v']))
        rw=m.get('recovery_windows') or []
        if rw: line.append('rec='+str([('%.2fus'%(w['recovery_s']*1e6) if w['recovery_s'] else ('LB%.2f'%(w['recovery_lower_bound_s']*1e6) if w['recovery_lower_bound_s'] else 'null')) for w in rw]))
        print(' ', line)

cd /workspace && python3 - <<'EOF'
import json,sys
sys.path.insert(0,'/workspace')
from gen import measure,build
def summ(rep,tag):
    print('==',tag)
    for c in rep.get('cases',[]):
        m=c.get('measurements',{})
        line=[c['case_id'], c['status']]
        if 'vout_v' in m: line.append('vout=%.4f'%m['vout_v'])
        if 'overhead_a' in m: line.append('ovh=%.1fuA'%(m['overhead_a']*1e6))
        ih=m.get('initial_hold')
        if ih: line.append('hold=[%.4f,%.4f]'%(ih['min_v'],ih['max_v']))
        en=m.get('envelope')
        if en: line.append('env=[%.4f,%.4f]'%(en['min_v'],en['max_v']))
        rw=m.get('recovery_windows') or []
        if rw: line.append('rec='+str([('%.2f'%(w['recovery_s']*1e6) if w['recovery_s'] else ('LB%.2f'%(w['recovery_lower_bound_s']*1e6) if w['recovery_lower_bound_s'] else '0')) for w in rw]))
        print('  ',line)
# rebuild vF to make sure dut.spice is vF, then validate/resources
base=dict(g1='VREF',g2='VFB',m5=1,wp=50,caps='RZ VG nm 20k\nCZ nm VOUT 5p\n')
open('/workspace/dut.spice','w').write(build(base))
summ(measure('vF_slc',base,analysis='nominal',pvt='SLC'),'vF SLC (slow,1.4,-20)')
summ(measure('vF_fhh',base,analysis='nominal',pvt='FHH'),'vF FHH (fast,1.8,85)')
summ(measure('vF_cl500n',base,analysis='nominal',pvt='N',),'vF N cl=200p (repeat)')
EOF

import json,sys
sys.path.insert(0,'/workspace')
import gen
from gen import build,run
def measure2(case,over,analysis='nominal',pvt='N',cl=None):
    open('/workspace/dut.spice','w').write(build(over))
    req={'action':'measure','analysis':analysis,'pvt':pvt,'experiment_id':case}
    if cl: req['cl']=cl
    open('/workspace/req.json','w').write(json.dumps(req))
    return run(['--request','/workspace/req.json'])
def summ(rep,tag):
    print('==',tag)
    for c in rep.get('cases',[]):
        m=c.get('measurements',{})
        line=[c['case_id'], c['status']]
        if 'vout_v' in m: line.append('vout=%.4f'%m['vout_v'])
        if 'overhead_a' in m: line.append('ovh=%.1fuA'%(m['overhead_a']*1e6))
        ih=m.get('initial_hold')
        if ih: line.append('hold=[%.4f,%.4f]'%(ih['min_v'],ih['max_v']))
        en=m.get('envelope')
        if en: line.append('env=[%.4f,%.4f]'%(en['min_v'],en['max_v']))
        rw=m.get('recovery_windows') or []
        if rw: line.append('rec='+str([('%.2f'%(w['recovery_s']*1e6) if w['recovery_s'] else ('LB%.2f'%(w['recovery_lower_bound_s']*1e6) if w['recovery_lower_bound_s'] else '0')) for w in rw]))
        print('  ',line)
caps='RZ VG nm 20k\nCZ nm VOUT 5p\n'
g1=dict(g1='VREF',g2='VFB',m5=1,wp=100,lp=0.15,mp=1,rb='470k',caps=caps)
summ(measure2('vG_N',g1),'vG N 200p')
summ(measure2('vG_SLC',g1,pvt='SLC'),'vG SLC')
summ(measure2('vG_FHH',g1,pvt='FHH'),'vG FHH')
g2=dict(g1,mp=2)
summ(measure2('vG2_SLC',g2,pvt='SLC'),'vG2 SLC (pass m=2)')
EOF

cd /workspace && python3 - <<'EOF'
import json,sys
sys.path.insert(0,'/workspace')
from gen import build,run
def measure2(case,over,analysis='nominal',pvt='N',cl=None):
    open('/workspace/dut.spice','w').write(build(over))
    req={'action':'measure','analysis':analysis,'pvt':pvt,'experiment_id':case}
    if cl: req['cl']=cl
    open('/workspace/req.json','w').write(json.dumps(req))
    return run(['--request','/workspace/req.json'])
def summ(rep,tag):
    print('==',tag)
    for c in rep.get('cases',[]):
        m=c.get('measurements',{})
        line=[c['case_id'], c['status']]
        if 'vout_v' in m: line.append('vout=%.4f'%m['vout_v'])
        if 'overhead_a' in m: line.append('ovh=%.1fuA'%(m['overhead_a']*1e6))
        en=m.get('envelope')
        if en: line.append('env=[%.4f,%.4f]'%(en['min_v'],en['max_v']))
        ih=m.get('initial_hold')
        if ih: line.append('hold_pp=%.4f'%(ih['peak_to_peak_v']))
        rw=m.get('recovery_windows') or []
        if rw: line.append('rec='+str([('%.2f'%(w['recovery_s']*1e6) if w['recovery_s'] else ('LB%.2f'%(w['recovery_lower_bound_s']*1e6) if w['recovery_lower_bound_s'] else '0')) for w in rw]))
        print('  ',line)
base=dict(g1='VREF',g2='VFB',m5=1,wp=100,lp=0.15,mp=1,rb='470k')
for tag,ov in [('vH',dict(caps='RZ VG nm 10k\nCZ nm VOUT 5p\n')),
                ('vI',dict(caps='RZ VG nm 20k\nCZ nm VOUT 2p\n')),
                ('vJ',dict(caps='RZ VG nm 20k\nCZ nm VOUT 5p\n',wn=2,wpu=6))]:
    for pvt in ['N','SLC','FHH']:
        summ(measure2(tag+'_'+pvt,dict(base,**ov),pvt=pvt),f'{tag} {pvt}')
EOF

sys.path.insert(0,'/workspace')
from gen import build,run
def measure2(case,over,analysis='nominal',pvt='N',cl=None):

    req={'action':'measure','analysis':analysis,'pvt':pvt,'experiment_id':case}
    if cl: req['cl']=cl
    open('/workspace/req.json','w').write(json.dumps(req))
    return run(['--request','/workspace/req.json'])
def summ(rep,tag):
    print('==',tag)
    for c in rep.get('cases',[]):
        m=c.get('measurements',{})
        line=[c['case_id'], c['status']]
        if 'vout_v' in m: line.append('vout=%.4f'%m['vout_v'])
        if 'overhead_a' in m: line.append('ovh=%.1fuA'%(m['overhead_a']*1e6))
        en=m.get('envelope')
        if en: line.append('env=[%.4f,%.4f]'%(en['min_v'],en['max_v']))
        ih=m.get('initial_hold')
        if ih: line.append('hold_pp=%.4f'%(ih['peak_to_peak_v']))
        rw=m.get('recovery_windows') or []
        if rw: line.append('rec='+str([('%.2f'%(w['recovery_s']*1e6) if w['recovery_s'] else ('LB%.2f'%(w['recovery_lower_bound_s']*1e6) if w['recovery_lower_bound_s'] else '0')) for w in rw]))
        print('  ',line)
base=dict(g1='VREF',g2='VFB',m5=1,wp=100,lp=0.15,mp=1,rb='470k',caps='RZ VG nm 10k\nCZ nm VOUT 5p\n')
# vK: fixed gate pull-up (guaranteed driver current)
summ(measure2('vK_SLC',dict(base,caps=base['caps']+'XMPU VG nbp VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=20 L=0.5 m=1\n'),pvt='SLC'),'vK SLC (+fixed pullup)')
summ(measure2('vK_FHH',dict(base,caps=base['caps']+'XMPU VG nbp VIN VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 W=20 L=0.5 m=1\n'),pvt='FHH'),'vK FHH')
# vL: stronger bias
summ(measure2('vL_SLC',dict(base,rb='330k'),pvt='SLC'),'vL SLC (rb330k)')
summ(measure2('vL_FHH',dict(base,rb='330k'),pvt='FHH'),'vL FHH')
# vM: smaller miller cap
summ(measure2('vM_SLC',dict(base,caps='RZ VG nm 10k\nCZ nm VOUT 2p\n'),pvt='SLC'),'vM SLC (10k2p)')
summ(measure2('vM_N',dict(base,caps='RZ VG nm 10k\nCZ nm VOUT 2p\n')),'vM N')

import json,sys
sys.path.insert(0,'/workspace')
from gen import build,run
def measure2(case,over,analysis='nominal',pvt='N',cl=None):
    open('/workspace/dut.spice','w').write(build(over))
    req={'action':'measure','analysis':analysis,'pvt':pvt,'experiment_id':case}
    if cl: req['cl']=cl
    open('/workspace/req.json','w').write(json.dumps(req))
    return run(['--request','/workspace/req.json'])
def summ(rep,tag):
    print('==',tag)
    for c in rep.get('cases',[]):
        m=c.get('measurements',{})
        line=[c['case_id'], c['status']]
        if 'vout_v' in m: line.append('vout=%.4f'%m['vout_v'])
        if 'overhead_a' in m: line.append('ovh=%.1fuA'%(m['overhead_a']*1e6))
        en=m.get('envelope')
        if en: line.append('env=[%.4f,%.4f]'%(en['min_v'],en['max_v']))
        ih=m.get('initial_hold')
        if ih: line.append('hold_pp=%.4f'%(ih['peak_to_peak_v']))
        rw=m.get('recovery_windows') or []
        if rw: line.append('rec='+str([('%.2f'%(w['recovery_s']*1e6) if w['recovery_s'] else ('LB%.2f'%(w['recovery_lower_bound_s']*1e6) if w['recovery_lower_bound_s'] else '0')) for w in rw]))
        print('  ',line)
b=dict(g1='VREF',g2='VFB',m5=1,wp=100,lp=0.15,mp=4,rb='470k')
for tag,ov,pvts in [('vP',dict(caps='RZ VG nm 10k\nCZ nm VOUT 5p\n'),['SLC','N','FHH']),

    for c in rep.get('cases',[]):

# vR: vH + 1p feedforward cap nb->VG

capsS='RZ VG nm 10k\nCZ nm VOUT 2p\nCFF nb VG 2p\n'

from gen import build,run
def measure2(case,over,analysis='nominal',pvt='N',cl=None):
    open('/workspace/dut.spice','w').write(build(over))

def summ(rep,tag):
    print('==',tag)
    for c in rep.get('cases',[]):
        m=c.get('measurements',{})
        line=[c['case_id'], c['status']]
        if 'vout_v' in m: line.append('vout=%.4f'%m['vout_v'])
        if 'overhead_a' in m: line.append('ovh=%.1fuA'%(m['overhead_a']*1e6))

        if en: line.append('env=[%.4f,%.4f]'%(en['min_v'],en['max_v']))
        rw=m.get('recovery_windows') or []
        if rw: line.append('rec='+str([('%.2f'%(w['recovery_s']*1e6) if w['recovery_s'] else ('LB%.2f'%(w['recovery_lower_bound_s']*1e6) if w['recovery_lower_bound_s'] else '0')) for w in rw]))
        print('  ',line)
# resistor pull-up driver: XMP2 removed -> wpu=0? use tiny W with m=1 ... instead scale: set XMP2 W=0.42 (min) -> too strong.
# simplest: keep XMP2 but make its W/L tiny (weak) and add RPU VIN VG
base=dict(g1='VREF',g2='VFB',m5=1,wp=100,lp=0.15,mp=1,rb='470k',wpu=0.42,lpu=1,mpu=1)
for tag,ov,pvts in [('vT',dict(caps='RPU VIN VG 47k\nRZ VG nm 10k\nCZ nm VOUT 5p\n'),['SLC','N','FHH']),
                    ('vU',dict(caps='RPU VIN VG 100k\nRZ VG nm 10k\nCZ nm VOUT 5p\n'),['SLC','N'])]:
    for pvt in pvts:
        summ(measure2(f'{tag}_{pvt}',dict(base,**ov),pvt=pvt),f'{tag} {pvt}')

import json,sys
sys.path.insert(0,'/workspace')
from gen import build,run
def measure2(case,over,analysis='nominal',pvt='N',cl=None):
    open('/workspace/dut.spice','w').write(build(over))
    req={'action':'measure','analysis':analysis,'pvt':pvt,'experiment_id':case}
    if cl: req['cl']=cl
    open('/workspace/req.json','w').write(json.dumps(req))
    return run(['--request','/workspace/req.json'])
def summ(rep,tag):
    print('==',tag)
    for c in rep.get('cases',[]):
        m=c.get('measurements',{})
        line=[c['case_id'], c['status']]
        if 'vout_v' in m: line.append('vout=%.4f'%m['vout_v'])
        if 'overhead_a' in m: line.append('ovh=%.1fuA'%(m['overhead_a']*1e6))
        en=m.get('envelope')
        if en: line.append('env=[%.4f,%.4f]'%(en['min_v'],en['max_v']))
        ih=m.get('initial_hold')
        if ih: line.append('hold_pp=%.3f'%(ih['peak_to_peak_v']*1000))
        rw=m.get('recovery_windows') or []
        if rw: line.append('rec='+str([('%.2f'%(w['recovery_s']*1e6) if w['recovery_s'] else ('LB%.2f'%(w['recovery_lower_bound_s']*1e6) if w['recovery_lower_bound_s'] else '0')) for w in rw]))
        print('  ',line)
# vV: resistor pull-up + EA with long L (lower offset sensitivity)
base=dict(g1='VREF',g2='VFB',m5=1,wp=100,lp=0.15,mp=1,rb='470k',wpu=0.42,lpu=1,mpu=1,
          w1=50,l1=0.5,w3=20,l3=1,
          caps='RPU VIN VG 47k\nRZ VG nm 10k\nCZ nm VOUT 5p\n')
for pvt in ['N','SLC','FHH']:
    summ(measure2('vV_'+pvt,dict(base),pvt=pvt),'vV '+pvt)

def measure2(case,over,analysis='nominal',pvt='N',cl=None):

def summ(rep,tag):

    for c in rep.get('cases',[]):
        m=c.get('measurements',{})
        line=[c['case_id'], c['status']]
        if 'vout_v' in m: line.append('vout=%.4f'%m['vout_v'])

        print('  ',line)

1:31:29

Submission complete and frozen. Summary of the work:

Final design (submitted)

Topology (dut.spice, .subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF):

  • Pass device: single PMOS, W=100 µm / L=0.15 µm, sized so it can carry 2 mA with only 200 mV of headroom at VIN=1.4 V (operating in triode at that corner, saturation at higher VIN).
  • Error amplifier: 5-transistor OTA with a PMOS input pair (VREF = 0.6 V is a natural common-mode for PMOS inputs), NMOS mirror load, PMOS tail. VFB feeds the mirror side and VREF the output side, which is the polarity that closes the loop negatively with the (non-inverting) gate driver.
  • Gate driver: push-pull NMOS/PMOS common-source stage (full 0.1→1.5 V gate range, unlike a source follower whose threshold level shift cannot reach the low gate voltage needed at VIN=1.4 V).
  • Compensation / fast path: series R-C (10 kΩ + 5 pF) from the pass gate to VOUT. It makes the gate node the loop's dominant pole through the pass device, contributes a LHP lead zero, kills the high-frequency driver gain (stability), and couples the output droop to the gate for a fast transient path. No large explicit caps otherwise (5 pF total ≪ 20 pF limit), MOS area 94 µm² ≪ 5000 µm².
  • Bias: resistor (470 k) + diode + mirror network; divider 106.5 k/100 k trimmed to absorb the OTA's systematic offset.

Measured results (native client measurements, both decaps):

  • DC: 1.1782–1.2138 V over all 9 PVT corners (band 1.176–1.224 V), load error < 1 mV between 50 µA and 2 mA.
  • Quiescent overhead at minimum load: 31–47 µA (limit 50 µA); Iref = 0; KCL residual ~1e-16 A.
  • Transients: initial holds flat (<0.5 mV pp), envelopes within ±5.3 % (limit ±8 %), recovery windows 0–0.42 µs (limit 0.5 µs) at all tested corners (N, SLC, SHC, FLH, FHH); the SLC corner was the critical one (driver gm collapses) and was fixed by making the pull-up device weak so the resistor-defined current dominates.

Major framework decisions: measured-only verification (no local PDK/ngspice), one architecture pass with a Python small-signal model to bound the stability problem, then ~50 native measurement batches iterating topology (direct drive → follower → mirror → inverter → resistor pull-up) and compensation.

SPICEFinal submission
